# SVC metrics at 5 Mbit/s — the `r5000` sitting (2026-11-04, in progress)

What was run: the pre-registered SVC metrics protocol on one more link, 5 Mbit/s, for the three DBT volumes `dbts_a1`, `dbts_b1` and `dbts_c1`, at 1× and 4× CPU, all 13 variants, 3 of 10 whole rounds so far.
What it shows: at 5 Mbit/s the layered variants put a first picture up in about a tenth of a second and a base on every slice in under a second, while HTJ2K's first exact slice takes seconds and its last about two minutes; the layered variants' time to exact stays near the shipped payload's, except where `p3-*` sends middles twice (tables 1, 2a, 4, 7).
What it rests on: three public DBT volumes only (`dbts_a1` 56 × 1142×1785 12-bit, `dbts_b1` 50 × 2560×3328 10-bit, `dbts_c1` 69 × 976×2513 12-bit), one 4-vCPU host and headless Chromium 153.0.8010.12; it claims nothing for other series, modalities, hosts or browsers.
What is left: the remaining rounds of this link if not 10; the links `r50000`, `r20000` with 2 % loss and with ±20 ms jitter; the breast ultrasound cine and ABUS rows; part 5's rule, which another session applies once every condition is in.

**Terms.** *SVC*, scalable video coding: one AV1 stream in layers, a small lossy **base** (a quarter-size picture), in `p3-*` a full-size lossy **middle**, and the **exact** (lossless) top. *Layer-major*: every slice's base is sent first, then every middle, then every exact frame. *`k2`*: the shipped single-layer AV1 payload; *`htj2k`*: HTJ2K, exact; *`res`*: HTJ2K's half-size prefix first; *`a2-*`*: a base sent beside `k2`; *`p2-*`*, *`p3-*`*: two or three layers with the exact top predicted from the layer below; *`lossy-*`*: the lossy layers alone, nothing exact. *t_P* / *t_ℓ,all*: seconds from the fill's issue until every slice has a picture at layer ℓ or better; *t_X*: until every slice is exact. *`VOID`*: a visit dropped because the relay's own timing broke the rig's 1 ms bound (p99). *A/A*: `htj2k-aa`, the same files as `htj2k` again, whose spread is the resolution below which no difference is reported. The full protocol is [`docs/av1/README.md`](../../../../../docs/av1/README.md) §The scalable preview as a tool, 10; how each number is made, [`README.md`](README.md) §SVC metrics.

**Method**, the first sitting's ([`svc-results.md`](svc-results.md)) with only the link changed: the same harness, builds and commands, rebuilt in a fresh container from the pinned sources (the rebuilt `svc-shapes.tsv`, every shape's bytes, exact-frame count and layer error, is byte-identical to the committed one), `--links r5000` (5 Mbit/s, 20 ms each way) in place of `none,r20000`, a fresh browser a visit, cells and variants in a Williams order (`lab/order.mjs`), each `run.mjs` process's temporary directory removed after its round. The container restarted under the run, keeping its disk and builds: 2026-11-04 near 23:30 UTC, 44 visits into round 2; 2026-11-05 near 00:05 UTC, 9 visits into round 2's rerun. Each time, the cut round's rows were set aside (not in the tables or the raw rows below) and that round run again whole. The offline metrics (3, the layer errors, and 5's offline decode) do not depend on the link and are the first sitting's; this file repeats none of them, and part 2's model below reads that sitting's `svc-decode.json`. No harness change was needed.

**Host.** 4 vCPU Xeon, 15 GB; its reported clock changed across restarts, so it is logged at each round's start: rounds 0–1 not recorded; round 2 2.10 GHz (the first sitting's: 2.1 GHz). The browser and server on three cores, the relay alone on the fourth. **At 5 Mbit/s the host does not saturate: every exact fill is link-bound**, at 1× and at 4×. The numbers, recomputed from the raw rows below at every push, over every visit with an exact layer, `VOID` ones included: wire bits ÷ the last byte's time; the last exact frame against the last byte; the median t_X at 4× ÷ at 1× per series and variant.

* wire rate of the exact fills: 4.91 Mbit/s median, 4.87–4.91, over 198 fills
* last exact frame after the last byte: 0.24 s median, 0.02–2.30 s
* t_X at 4× ÷ at 1×: 1.001–1.008 over 33 series × variants
* `VOID`: 37 of 198 fills with an exact layer, 8 of 36 lossy-only; relay p99 0.66 ms median, max 5.16

`VOID` visits by round, of 78: round 0 25, round 1 17, round 2 3. At 20 Mbit/s the first sitting dropped 2–8 of 130 a cell. Why the rate changes between rounds is *unverified*: the rounds with many ran before the first restart, on a machine whose clock was not recorded. That CPU load is not the cause is *derived* from 4× moving no fill's time beyond the figure above. Each cell reports its n, and the cells' n is what this sitting can claim.

**Exactness**, over every visit, `VOID` ones included, per cell in the list at the end of the tables: every exact frame against its checksum, every base and middle against native dav1d at its operating point, previews after their exact picture (`late`), lossy layers posted as exact (`stray`), and stall-flushes (a unit at the worker's 10 000 ms bound). The checks are the first sitting's, each mutated there ([`svc-mutations.jsonl`](svc-mutations.jsonl)); this sitting adds no check and so mutates none. **Totals: 11 550/11 550 exact frames equal to their checksums; 9 450 bases and 4 200 middles equal to native dav1d; 0 late, 0 stray, 0 stall-flushes.**
